unit-tested. Nothing is activated; production behaviour is unchanged.**

**Gates on remaining services work (not buildable now):**

## Current Production State
- **Supabase:** project `den-seje-app` (ref `tjzbehwfagiwpwodsgwg`), region eu-west-1, plan **Pro**.
- **Data:** 31 profiles; 5 with equipped cosmetics; 2 with an explicit legacy hairstyle.
- **Frontend:** ~~Vercel (`den-seje-app-frontend.vercel.app`), auto-deploys from GitHub `main`.~~
  > **STATUS UPDATE — 2026-08-07: HOSTING IS MID-MIGRATION AND THE VERCEL HOST IS DOWN.**
  > Vercel Hobby was paused after ~3M edge requests against the plan's 1M limit; the production URL
  > returns **`402 Payment Required`** on every page. A Cloudflare Workers (Static Assets) deployment
  > is **prepared and deployable** (`den-seje-app-frontend.christ-moeller.workers.dev`, asset-only,
  > allowlist build) and is what CI now tests against via the `PROD_BASE_URL` repository variable —
  > but it is **not activated for users**: DNS, custom domain and the Supabase redirect-URL list are
  > unchanged, and `tests/helpers.ts` still defaults to the Vercel address on purpose.
  > **Until the host switch is completed, users on the Vercel domain get 402.**
  > Canonical: [`HOSTING.md`](./HOSTING.md) — do not restate hosting details here.
- **Avatar shown to users (as of 2026-06-15):** **LEGACY** render. C2 built but not active.
  > **STATUS UPDATE — 2026-06-30 (Section 157AB): SUPERSEDED.** `AVATAR_V2 = true` is now **live in
  > production** (commit `52f8365`, 2026-06-25). The **C2 render path is the default** for users.
  > However it still renders **flat placeholder SVGs**, **not** the Northstar Master raster — Master
  > asset production + wiring remain **future work** (`docs/167a-master-asset-raster-wiring-plan.md`).
  > Canonical: [`AVATAR_SYSTEM.md`](./AVATAR_SYSTEM.md) §2.

## Art Direction Decision (NEW)
- **SVG-only is REJECTED as the final art strategy.** Flat SVG cannot deliver the
  desired avatar art quality (large expressive eyes + premium anime-inspired finish).
- **Direction:** **Hybrid Raster** — produce the North Star character as WebP layers
  in the existing format-agnostic pipeline; reuse DB / identity / slot / z-model /
  AVATAR_V2 (Section 163A). North Star Avatar v1.0 is the permanent visual target.
- **Pipeline LOCKED (ADR-163D / D-013…D-019):** WebP assets · hair color = hybrid
  luminance-map tint · eye color = iris-base tint (fixed highlight) · skin tone =
  separate base assets · hybrid loading · immutable versioned cache · mobile perf
  budget. Architecture (DB/identity/slots/z-model/AVATAR_V2) unchanged.

## Avatar Layer Model (Hybrid Raster — current)
Ordered render layers (reuse the existing z-model):
1. **Base body** (skin + neutral underlayer + head, NO face) — per skin tone — z 0–2
2. **Face/Expression** (brows, nose, mouth, multiply blush — NO eyes, NO skin) — z 3
3. **Eyes** (separate, tintable iris + fixed highlight — see D-012) — **z 4 (D-030)**
4. **Blink** (eyelid, engine) — z 5
5. **Hair** (luminance map + tint; `hair-northstar-v1`, D-031) — z 40
6. **Cosmetics** (equipped_slots at C2_LAYER_Z)

> This model **supersedes Section 163A's "eyes embedded in Face/Expression"** —
> eyes are now their own layer (D-012 / ADR-163B). Render stack locked by D-030;
> full decomposition spec in `docs/adr/ADR-163F-raster-asset-spec.md`.
>
> **MVP base (D-040):** the layer model above is the **full raster target**, but for MVP
> the **Tier-0 base is `Northstar Master.png` as-is** (fixed default avatar) + accessory
> overlays only. The decomposed neutral base + per-skin-tone/face/eyes/hair layers are a
> **deferred upgrade**, not an MVP blocker.

## Major Decisions (register)
| ID | Decision |
|---|---|
| D-001 | Avatar direction = **C2 Base Avatar Premium** (human chibi/anime kid), chosen in a bake-off over creature/axolotl, chibi study-hero, geometric mascot, legacy redesign. |
| D-002 | Hair = identity; Hair Color = identity; Companion = not MVP; Badge = not MVP. |
| D-003 | Hair Color = inline SVG + CSS tokens (`--hair-base` / `--hair-shadow`). |
| D-004 | Asset spec: viewBox `0 0 160 240`; LOCKED anchors head `cx80 cy50 r30`, eyes `cx68/92 cy47`; flat (R1–R5, no gradients). |
| D-005 | C2 base reuses the legacy anchors (preserve blink/expression) → C2 shipped as a same-proportion restyle. |
| D-006 | Free staging not recommended (migration drift); use Supabase Branching (Pro) or direct prod apply. |
| D-007 | Direct prod apply of 155E/155F (assessed SAFE, executed, verified). |
| D-008 | Z-model reform (159B): deterministic `C2_LAYER_Z`; expr z=3, blink z=5, hair z=40. |
| D-009 | Cosmetics parity-first (render legacy assets in C2); flat redesign progressive. |
| D-010 | 159D: static neutral expression in C2 shop preview (face parity). |
| **D-011** | **SVG-only rejected as final art direction; move to hybrid/raster pipeline.** |
| D-011b | Hybrid Raster architecture: North Star as WebP layers in the existing pipeline; reuse DB/identity/slots/z-model/AVATAR_V2 (163A). North Star v1.0 = permanent visual target. |
| **D-012** | **Eye system = a SEPARATE, tintable, cosmetic-capable layer** (ADR-163B, Option C). Iris is tint-controlled (eye color = token, free); the eye layer supports future eye cosmetics, eye rarity, magic/rare eyes, glasses, masks, blink and the emotion system. **Supersedes 163A's "eyes embedded in Face/Expression".** Rationale: multiple eye colors + eye cosmetics + rarity would otherwise force a combinatorial asset explosion (expression × color × variant) and a future rewrite; a separate tintable layer is the robust, future-proof choice. |
| D-013 | Asset format = **WebP** (PNG fallback only if needed). Canonical 2:3 raster master 1024×1536 → served WebP 512×768; anchors mapped proportionally from the 160×240 geometry. (ADR-163D) |
| D-014 | Hair color = **hybrid**: canvas multiply-tint of a neutral luminance map (8 colors free as tokens) + hand-painted variant override for problem colors. (ADR-163D) |
| D-015 | Eye color = **tint the iris-base only**; sclera/pupil/glossy highlight stay fixed; rare/magic eyes = iris-swap or eye-effect overlay in the eye slot. (ADR-163D) |
| D-016 | Skin tone = **separate base assets** per `body_type × skin_tone` (cel-shaded skin is not reliably runtime-tintable; body owns skin). (ADR-163D) |
| D-017 | Asset loading = **hybrid**: eager preload of the user's own avatar; lazy-load shop catalog + other avatars. (ADR-163D) |
| D-018 | Cache = **immutable, versioned assets + manifest**; invalidation via filename version (never mutate a shipped asset). (ADR-163D) |
| D-019 | **Mobile-first performance budget**: first-paint < 100ms, full composite < 250ms, total avatar < ~350KB, decoded memory < ~15MB. (ADR-163D) |
| D-020 | **MVP = one Neutral North Star character**; body-type system preserved architecturally (additive later, no rewrite). (ADR-163F) |
| D-021 | **Eye granularity = 2 files**: `iris` (tintable) + `fixed` (sclera/lash/shape + fixed highlight). MVP scope; cosmetic/rarity capability (D-012) preserved. (ADR-163F) |
| D-022 | **Face/Expression layer = tone-agnostic**: no opaque skin shading (base owns skin), blush = `multiply`, carries brows/nose/mouth; one face set shared across skin tones. (ADR-163F) |
| D-023 | **Blink = WebP eyelid assets per skin tone** (eyelid shows skin → skin-bearing layer). (ADR-163F) |
| D-024 | **Expression MVP = positive only**: neutral, happy, curious, focused, determined, surprised, proud (7). `sad`/`angry` excluded — permanent **never-negative policy** (resolves OQ-6). (ADR-163F) |
| D-025 | **Hair compression ignored in MVP**; contract hook reserved: `hair_state = full \| compressed` (MVP produces `full`). (ADR-163F) |
| D-026 | **Cosmetic recolor = hybrid**: MVP = baked assets; tint = future opt-in (no cosmetic tint pipeline in MVP). (ADR-163F) |
| D-027 | **Asset canvas = full canvas, all layers**, transparent padding; no cropped/trimmed per-layer assets (one shared coordinate space). (ADR-163F) |
| D-028 | **North Star Master v1.0 = `medium` skin-tone token** (internal token, not a subjective colour reading; no new tone tokens / no rename now). (ADR-163F, 164A) |
| D-029 | **Neutral body-underlayer = a derived production asset** cut from the master; the master's sweater/jeans/sneakers are reference art (future cosmetic set), never baked into `base`. (ADR-163F, 164A) |
| D-030 | **Eye layer z-index = 4**; render stack locked: base z0–2 · face z3 · **eyes z4** · blink z5 · hair z40 · cosmetics `C2_LAYER_Z`. Fills the eyes gap left by D-008. (ADR-163F, 164A) |
| D-031 | **North Star hairstyle token = `hair-northstar-v1`** (one approved hairstyle; produced as a neutral luminance map, brown = default tint). (ADR-163F, 164A) |
| **D-032** | **North Star source-of-truth split (164B-prep).** `assets/avatar/reference/Northstar Master.png` is the **SOLE authoritative geometric source** for 164B decomposition — proportions, head/body ratio, pose, hair silhouette, face, eyes, rendering style and character identity all derive from it. `Northstar Master - reference.png` is **downgraded to an outfit-style reference ONLY** (neutral clothing direction: plain t-shirt / plain trousers / plain sneakers). It **MUST NOT** be used as a source of truth for proportions, body height, hair shape, eye size, facial structure or pose. **On any conflict between the two images, `Northstar Master.png` always wins.** Rationale: four regeneration attempts of the companion all drifted taller/leaner with an altered hair silhouette — proportions cannot be locked via regeneration, so geometry is taken directly from the frozen Master. (164B-prep, 2026-06-15) |
| **D-033** | **Base production method = manual paint-over, NOT AI (164C — Base Production Method Pivot).** AI generation/inpainting **cannot reliably** produce `body-neutral-medium-v1`: it repeatedly introduces **proportion + identity drift** (confirmed across four companion regenerations **and** an explicit edit/inpaint-mode attempt — all drifted taller/leaner with altered hair and face, D-032). **Decision:** AI-generated / AI-inpainted images **MUST NOT** be used as production base assets; they may be used **only as visual outfit references**. `body-neutral-medium-v1` must be produced from a **manually controlled layered source / manual paint-over over `Northstar Master.png`** (Master = geometry, D-032), validated by the 164B.3 base-coherence gate. | **D-049** | **Protect-mask contamination correction — REQUIRED before D-048 can be effective (2026-07-13).** **Finding:** the current identity-lock `protect-mask.png` **contains clothing pixels**. Audited against the D-042 candidate in the correct frame (115,660 protect px total): **legitimate skin/identity 92,521 px (80.0 %)**, **orange sleeve stripes 8,220 px (7.1 %)**, **green sweater remnants 2,462 px (2.1 %)**, line art 6,429 px (5.6 %), other 6,028 px (5.2 %). **10,682 px of GARMENT is currently inside the identity-lock.** The mask appears to have been derived by **skin-colour detection**, and the orange sleeve stripes — whose hue is skin-adjacent — were swept in. **Consequence:** the orange stripes and green remnants survived every earlier pass **not because they are true identity, but because the protect mask is contaminated**. While this mask stands, **no donor-lift, no masked inpaint and no manual paint-over can ever remove them** (protect-diff = 0 forbids touching them). **This is the same class of defect as D-046**, where the wristbands survived because of mask logic (a mask gap), not because of genuine identity — here it is the inverse (wrongly *inside* the mask). **Decision:** produce a **review-first, deterministic (non-AI) protect-mask carve proposal** — `protect-mask-v2` — that removes clothing-coloured pixels from the protect mask while preserving true identity. **PRESERVE: head, blank face, ears, jaw, neck skin, hands, and all real skin/identity line art. REMOVE from protect: orange sleeve stripes, green sweater remnants, non-identity garment blobs on the arms. | **D-059** | **Neutral R2 base alpha fringe PARTIALLY remediated at runtime-asset level — trousers/shoes cleared, arms only ~34 % reduced; PARTIAL and TEMPORARY, the permanent fix belongs in the source art (2026-07-20; scope corrected 2026-07-20 after post-merge visual verification — see (11)).** **(1) SYMPTOM.** The promoted neutral R2 base showed a light/grey halo on dark backgrounds along the forearms, hands, trouser outer sides and shoe outlines. **(2) MEASURED CAUSE — three contributions, not one.** **(a) 655 FULLY OPAQUE near-white matte pixels** painted into the artwork on the silhouette's outer rim — the dominant contribution; **no alpha-domain operation can reach them**. **(b) 1,047 light PARTIAL-ALPHA pixels** in the antialias band. **(c) 85 px of DETACHED white debris** in 24 components floating 1–3 px off the arms. **This is the same family as the D-056 §7 inherited alpha/matte halo, now measured at the runtime-asset level.** **(3) WHY THE EARLIER ATTEMPT FAILED — recorded so it is not repeated.** A first pass (`build-r2-alpha-decontaminate.mjs`) repaired only RGB of pixels with `a < 255`. It rewrote ~33,900 pixels with **zero visible change**: it could not touch (a) at all, and for (b) it **sampled the still-white opaque rim and propagated the matte straight back out**. **Pass order is load-bearing** — the opaque rim must be cleaned *before* the antialias band is decontaminated. **(4) METHOD.** `tools/avatar/build-r2-alpha-rim-fix.mjs` — deterministic, **non-AI**, four passes (opaque matte rim recolour → partial-alpha decontamination → detached-debris clear → `a=0` RGB decontamination), encoded **`-lossless -exact`**. The light test is **relative to the adjacent body colour**, so genuinely light artwork (white shoe soles, laces) is left untouched. **No erosion, no dilation, no blur, no new outline.** **(5) VERIFIED against the re-decoded WebP (hard-fail invariants).** Main silhouette pixel set **unchanged** (`mainPxDiff=0`) · main silhouette alpha **unchanged** (`mainAlphaDiff=0`) · interior RGB **unchanged** (`mainInteriorRgbDiff=0`) · alpha writes **85 px, exclusively detached debris** · dimensions **512×768** · **determinism**: two independent encodes byte-identical · **idempotent**: re-running on the promoted asset finds `matteRimPx=0, partialAA=0, detached=0`. **(6) CHANGED ASSET.** `assets/avatar-r2/base/body-neutral-medium-v2.webp` — **old** SHA-256 `0DEA234893361A1D143636178643C3BD8119F1861F398438654E980BD8AC7182` · **23,594 bytes** → **new** SHA-256 `3A30D8C7BC29A4813E9F4F2902FED26235B3458A56F733C11067559968DA4F37` · **66,526 bytes**. **(7) ENCODING DEVIATION — deliberate and measured.** The `167a` promotion contract's lossy `-q 90` path was **measured to alter 27,526 opaque pixels per re-encode generation**, which would silently undo the repair. **`-lossless -exact` is the only encoding that keeps every non-repaired pixel byte-exact.** Cost: **23.0 KB → 65.0 KB (+42 KB)** — to be weighed against the ADR-163D ~350 KB total-avatar budget before any further layer receives the same treatment. **(8) NOT CHANGED.** `face-neutral-v1` (30 matte rim px) and `eyes-neutral-fixed-v1` (46 px) were **measured but deliberately left unchanged**: stacked on the repaired base their rim sits over opaque skin, not against the background, and shows **no separate visible fringe**. **(9) BOUNDARIES.** **`AVATAR_R2` remains `false`.** **No runtime, `R2_MANIFEST`, blink, database or golden changes.** `Northstar Master.png` (D-032), the D-057 base source and the D-058 protect contract are **untouched** — this repair was applied to the **runtime WebP only**, not to any source or countersigned Gate-2 artefact. **(10) STATUS — PARTIAL AND TEMPORARY.** This is a **runtime remediation, not a source fix**, and it is **incomplete** (see (11)). The matte rim is **painted into the source art**; the durable correction belongs in the master raster (167a track) and remains later work. **The original `167a` Gate-3 countersign and its `0DEA2348…` hash are PRESERVED as historical record** — see the note in `docs/167a-phase2-gate3-neutral-asset-promotion.md` §3. **(11) SCOPE CORRECTION — the fringe is NOT fully removed (recorded 2026-07-20, post-merge).** A visual verification of the merged asset on all four real surfaces under the R2 opt-in (fixture-intercepted; no backend contact) showed a **still-visible light fringe along both forearms and hands**. Measured light pixels (`luma > 200`) in the promoted asset vs the original: **trousers (y 560–640): 115 → 0 (cleared)**; **arms (y 330–560): 1,565 → 1,038 (only ~34 % reduced)**. **ROOT CAUSE of the shortfall:** `RIM_DIST = 2` made only the outermost two pixel rings eligible, so matte structures **thicker than ~4 px keep an "interior"** that the rim pass treats as legitimate body colour — and that slab's own outer ring is then compared against its own white interior, giving `Δluma ≈ 0`, so it is not flagged either. Depth histogram of the promoted asset: **779 light px at d ≤ 2** (eligible) vs **26,093 at d > 2** (never eligible; most of that total is the legitimately white shoes). **Thin matte (trouser sides, shoe outlines, hand contours) was therefore fully repaired; the thick matte slabs beside the arms survived.** **Sections (1)–(9) remain factually accurate as written** — every invariant, count and hash still holds, and the change is an unambiguous improvement with no regression, no new outline and no shape change. **What is corrected here is the SCOPE claim only:** this decision must not be read as "the alpha fringe is removed". **A complete fix requires a different detection strategy** (component-based identification of matte slabs outside the silhouette, not a rim-distance test) **and is deliberately NOT attempted here** — the owner decision is to let the residual stand and close it at source in the 167a master-raster work. Preserves D-032/D-033/D-042…D-058. (PR #96) (2026-07-20) |

| **D-060** | **Permanent source-art fix of the neutral R2 arm fringe is BLOCKED by the D-058 protect contract — a protect-diff = 0 fix is not possible; escalated as a separate owner protect/alpha decision (2026-07-21).** This is the D-059 §11 follow-up: attempt the durable fix at the SOURCE (not the runtime WebP). **Outcome: FAIL at the protect gate — no source candidate was built, no protect exception was created.** **(1) MANDATE.** Re-measure the D-059 residual arm/hand fringe at source scale, project it against the D-058 protect contract, and build source candidates ONLY if a meaningful protect-diff = 0 fix is possible. **(2) FILES (all verified byte-identical to their register hashes; none modified).** Northstar Master `assets/avatar/reference/Northstar Master.png` (sha `2CA10EF8…607A21`, 1,192,451 B, D-032) · D-057 neutral base source `assets/avatar/reference/neutral-base-v1-gate2-d053.png` (sha `2CB93EE0…8AFE4B`, 331,506 B) · D-058 protect contract `assets/avatar/reference/gate2-protect-mask-v2.1.png` (sha `302324B7…F692F5`, 13,715 B) · runtime base `assets/avatar-r2/base/body-neutral-medium-v2.webp` (sha `3A30D8C7…8DA4F37`, 66,526 B, D-059). **(3) RESIDUAL MEASUREMENT (source 1024×1536, arm band y 660–1180).** Deterministic, non-AI detector — NOT luma-alone: a pixel counts only if it is `alpha>0`, on the outer silhouette edge (`distToTransparent ≤ 4`), reads light on the dark app bg (`compLuma > 200`), AND is `> 40` luma lighter than the nearest interior body colour it borders (so skin, grey tee and dark trousers each set their own local reference; legit mid-tone art and the white shoes are excluded). **Total visible arm residual = 3,488 px.** By category `[total / of-which-inside-protect]`: skin-edge 14/13 · tee/sleeve-edge 961/885 · hand/wrist 858/664 · detached-debris 409/408 · legit-highlight 0/0 · uncertain 1,246/1,235. **Physical basis:** only `alpha>0` pixels render, so the visible fringe is the silhouette's own light antialias/matte rim — not invisible fully-transparent void pixels. **(4) PROTECT OVERLAP — the decisive finding.** **3,205 of 3,488 residual px (91.9 %) lie INSIDE the protect zone; only 283 px (8.1 %) are outside** (282 main-figure edge + 1 detached; outside-protect distribution underarm 77 / wrist 18 / hand 188). This is the same conflict D-056/D-058 recorded for the GLOBAL halo (2,011 px, 1,557 = 77 % inside protect), now measured specifically for the arms and found **even more locked (91.9 %)**. **(5) WHY A protect-diff = 0 FIX IS NOT POSSIBLE.** A protect-diff = 0 change can touch at most the 283 outside-protect px (8.1 %); cleaning only those leaves **91.9 % of the visible fringe untouched** — exactly the "inconsistent, half-cleaned edge" the owner explicitly REJECTED in D-056 (where the analogous 454-px non-protect-only cleanup was forbidden). Per the binding rule (D-049/D-058) no pixel inside protect may change without a separate owner decision, and this investigation is forbidden from creating a protect exception itself. **Therefore: FAIL. No A/B/C source candidate built; no "best" candidate selected (fail-closed).** **(6) BASELINE CONFIRMED (render scale, §3).** Reproduced on all four real surfaces under the R2 opt-in (fixture-intercepted; 0 backend calls): the arm/hand fringe is still visible at 320 px, fades at 64, effectively gone at 48/32; **trousers and shoes remain clean — no regression since D-059**; no page/console errors. **(7) RECOMMENDATION — owner decision required.** The durable fix needs the separate protect/alpha decision D-056 deferred. Options: **(a)** approve a narrow protect exception for the arm-edge antialias band (only path to a source-level clean; needs a new D-decision); **(b)** ACCEPT the residual permanently — visible only at large sizes, invisible at 48/32, and `AVATAR_R2 = false` means no user sees it (lowest risk, recommended now); **(c)** defer to a full 167a master-raster redraw where protect is re-derived from scratch (right long-term home). Not chosen here — this entry records the conflict and stops for owner review. **(8) OWNER-REVIEW PACK (gitignored).** `tools/avatar/build/source-arm-fringe-owner-review/` — `00-contact-sheet.png`, `src-protect-overlay.png`, `residual-mask-overlay.png` (red = inside protect / green = outside), `protect-overlap-overlay.png`, `residual-mask.png`, `zoom-src-{Lforearm,Lhand,Rforearm}.png`, `surface-{avatar,hub,quiz,shop}-page.png`, `baseline-sizes-{dark,light}.png`, `baseline-arm-zooms.png`, `manifest.json`, and five deterministic non-AI analysis tools. **(9) BOUNDARIES — nothing touched.** No image edited; **protect mask unchanged** (sha `302324B7…`); **D-057 source unchanged** (sha `2CB93EE0…`); **Master unchanged** (sha `2CA10EF8…`); **runtime WebP unchanged** (sha `3A30D8C7…`); no candidate promoted or committed; no `R2_MANIFEST`, runtime, blink, database or golden change; **`AVATAR_R2` stays `false`; Gate 3 remains PAUSED.** Preserves D-032/D-033/D-042…D-059. It is the intersection of (measured residual) ∩ (arm/wrist/hand outer-edge near-white matte, luma ≥ 215) ∩ (D-058 protect), gated to the arm columns and excluding face/head/ears/jaw/hair/eyes/torso/inner-arm/fingers/nails/skin-shadow/line-art/tee-highlights. **It is a NARROW write-exception for THIS fix only; it does NOT replace, edit or weaken D-058, which remains byte-identical** (sha `302324B7…`). **(4) METHOD (deterministic, non-AI).** `tools/avatar/build-r2-arm-fringe-fix.mjs` (tracked; hash-locks its D-057/D-058/D-059 inputs and hard-fails on any mismatch or invariant breach; writes production assets only with `--promote`). Connected matte → RGB recoloured to the nearest interior SKIN colour (region-aware BFS from skin only; never samples matte/tee/trouser); detached matte → alpha cleared (not the main figure, not erosion). **Main-figure alpha is unchanged → no erosion, no shape change, no new outline; line-art, fingers and identity untouched.** **(5) NEW TRACKED SOURCE.** `assets/avatar/reference/neutral-base-v2-armfringe.png` · **SHA-256 `347A258FB962DB38…`** · **1024×1536** · **328,373 B** · owner-approved arm-fringe source built on the D-057 base. **The historical D-057 source `neutral-base-v1-gate2-d053.png` is preserved byte-identical** (sha `2CB93EE0…`); this is a NEW version, not a rewrite. Source verify: **alphaMainDiff = 0 · protect-diff outside exception = 0 · outsideExceptionDiff = 0 · deterministic (two builds byte-identical).** **(6) RUNTIME PROMOTION.** `assets/avatar-r2/base/body-neutral-medium-v2.webp` — **old** `3A30D8C7…8DA4F37` · 66,526 B (D-059) → **new** `28765EEA616DD92B…` · **67,174 B** (`-lossless -exact`). Built as the CURRENT D-059 runtime + the arm-exception fix (NOT re-derived from source), so **D-059's trouser/shoe result is preserved byte-exact**: only the 376 arm-exception px changed (`outsideMaskDiff = 0`), main-figure alpha unchanged, lossless roundtrip pixel-identical, deterministic. Budget: 67.2 KB base layer, within the ADR-163D ~350 KB total-avatar budget. **(7) RENDER VERIFICATION.** All four surfaces (avatar/hub/quiz/index/shop) under the R2 opt-in (fixture-intercepted, 0 backend) served the promoted asset **byte-identical to the approved Candidate B** (`28765EEA…` on every surface). The clear white matte along the forearms/wrists/hands is removed/reduced as in Candidate B; no new dark outline; no shape or identity change; bukser and sko unchanged from D-059. **(8) ACCEPTED RESIDUAL — this result must NOT be described as "completely removed" or "perfect".** The owner accepted that faint sub-215 specks and a minor light patch at the wrist/trouser transition may remain; Candidate B is visually approved as SUFFICIENT for this phase, not flawless. A fully-clean result would need a wider exception or the 167a master-raster redraw (later work). **(9) BOUNDARIES.** **`AVATAR_R2` stays `false`.** No `R2_MANIFEST`, runtime-code, blink, database, golden or R2-activation change. `Northstar Master.png` (D-032), the D-057 source and the D-058 protect contract are all byte-identical. The write-exception applies only to this fix and grants no general writable region. Preserves D-032/D-033/D-042…D-060. (branch `fix/avatar-r2-arm-fringe-protect-exception`) (2026-07-21) |

| **D-062** | **Atomic R2 asset-load fallback — a failed mandatory R2 layer now drops the WHOLE avatar to a complete C2 render (closes activation-audit F1) (2026-07-22).** **(1) THE F1 GAP.** The activation-readiness audit found that `composeR2Layers`/the manifest gate chose R2 BEFORE any image loaded, so a 404/decode failure on a mandatory R2 layer left a **broken partial R2 stack** (broken base `<img>` + floating face/hair), never a C2 fallback. **(2) FIX — atomic preload/decode gate (`js/avatar-render-c2.js`, `mountC2Avatar`).** When R2 is chosen, every MANDATORY layer (base, blush, face, iris, eyes-fixed, hair) is **preloaded + `.decode()`-ed off-DOM** before the stack is made visible. On ANY mandatory failure (HTTP 4xx/5xx, `img.onerror`, `decode()` reject, `naturalWidth===0`, empty/invalid URL, missing manifest entry after the R2 choice), the whole stack **atomically falls back to the complete existing C2/SVG path** — never a partial R2 stack, never a broken image, never a visible base-without-face / face-without-base window (the prior render stays until the complete new stack is ready). **(3) OPTIONAL vs MANDATORY.** Safe cosmetic overlays (aura/back) are OPTIONAL: a failed overlay is dropped individually and the R2 base survives — it does NOT collapse the base to C2. Documented + unit-tested. **(4) RACE / ABORT (§5).** A per-element mount generation (WeakMap) is claimed synchronously at call start; after every await the mount checks it still owns the element and returns `"aborted"` without touching the DOM if a newer mount superseded it. A stale/aborted load can never overwrite a newer render; no double C2 mount; no duplicate blink layer. **(5) BLINK / LIVING CONTRACT.** `mountC2Avatar` now returns the render path actually mounted (`"r2"|"c2"|"aborted"`) and stamps `rootEl.dataset.avatarRenderPath`. `blinkConfigFor(identity, r2ActiveOverride?)` takes an optional override; each surface (avatar.html, hub.html, app.js) keys BOTH the expression-overlay gate and the blink profile off the ACTUAL mounted path — so an R2 asset-load fallback gets the C2 living contract (expression ON, C2 lids), never R2 lids on a C2 base. Blink starts only after the complete mount; the fallback does not create a duplicate blink layer (engine's single `#avatar-blink-layer`, `destroy()` clears its timer). **(6) LOGGING (§9).** One controlled, non-sensitive `console.warn` per failed mount (reason only — no identity, student-id or token); no repeat loops. **(7) TESTS.** New `tests/unit/avatar-r2-atomic-fallback.test.mjs` (14 cases: all-load→R2, base/face/eyes/hair fail→C2, decode-failure→C2, multi-fail→one C2 mount, optional-overlay-drop keeps base, newer-mount-wins abort, forceC2, null root, blinkConfigFor override). New `tests/avatar-r2-fallback.spec.ts` (fixture-intercepted, self-served, 0 backend contact): clean→R2 and base-404→C2 / face-404→C2 on avatar/hub/index — passing on Chromium + Firefox + WebKit. `npm run test:unit` 69/69; existing avatar/living/golden suites unchanged (C2 default byte-for-byte identical). **(8) BOUNDARIES.** **`AVATAR_R2` stays `false`** — this is fallback hardening, not activation. No asset, `R2_MANIFEST`, database, migration, RLS, Edge Function, golden or pilot change. Only render-path code (`js/avatar-render-c2.js`, `js/avatar-layers.js` blinkConfigFor signature, avatar.html/hub.html/app.js engine gates) + two test files. **Closes F1 only; F2–F5 from the audit remain open.** Preserves D-032/D-033/D-042…D-061. (branch `fix/avatar-r2-atomic-load-fallback`) (2026-07-22) |

| **D-063** | **Deterministic R2 blink-frame goldens — open/closed lids are now visually protected on exact R2 neutral-medium (closes activation-audit F5). Also fixes a latent D-062 regression that stopped avatar.html blink from mounting (2026-07-22).** F5 flagged that R2 blink was unit-tested and mounted but its OPEN vs CLOSED lids were never visually compared or golden-protected. **(1) DETERMINISTIC TEST SEAM.** `BlinkEngine.forceFrame('open'|'closed')` (`js/avatar-blink-engine.js`): clears any pending auto-blink, builds the lid layer if absent (so it works under prefers-reduced-motion, which otherwise suppresses it), pins both lids to a STATIC transition-free frame, and sets the layer's inline `display:block` to override the reduced-motion `#avatar-blink-layer{display:none}` CSS. It uses THIS engine's mode geometry/fill, so a golden captures the engine's REAL R2 lids (Option-A geometry `cx 66.7/90.6, cy 60.3, rx 8.91, ry 9.06`; measured medium fill `#FEC183`), not a replica. The constructor self-registers `window.__avatarBlinkEngine` as a test handle **GATED behind an explicit `window.__AVATAR_TEST__ === true` signal** (set by the harness via `addInitScript`); **production never sets that flag, so no blink-engine global is exposed to users.** `destroy()` clears the global when it still points at the destroyed engine, and a remount overwrites it with the new engine. **Both are test-only: nothing calls them in production, so auto-blink timing and user-facing behaviour are unchanged** (reduced-motion users still see no blink). *(Gate added as a pre-merge review fix — pre-merge review flagged the ungated global as SHOULD-BE-GATED; no golden regeneration needed, the render output is byte-identical.)* **(2) LATENT D-062 REGRESSION FIXED.** The atomic R2 preload (D-062) delays when `mountC2Avatar` appends its layers; on avatar.html `initAvatarLifeEngines()` ran at top level right after `await loadAll()` (which fires the async render without awaiting), so it saw no layers yet and bailed → **the blink/expression/presence engines were never created on avatar.html**. Fixed by initialising the engines at the end of `renderAvatarC2()` once its layers exist (guarded against double-init, mirroring hub.html), with the top-level call kept as a guarded fallback. hub.html/app.js already init post-mount and were unaffected. **(3) GOLDENS (chromium; win32 + linux).** `tests/avatar-r2-blink-frames.spec.ts` — SELF-SERVED + FIXTURE-INTERCEPTED (0 backend, no shared-student mutation; uses the branch code that carries the seam, which production does not yet have). Baselines: `r2-avatar-page-blink-open`, `r2-avatar-page-blink-closed`, `r2-hub-avatar-blink-closed`, `r2-quiz-avatar-blink-closed`. Determinism: reduced motion freezes breathing/expression + suppresses auto-blink, leaving only the pinned lid frame. **(4) CONTRACT VERIFIED (all browsers where functional).** open→closed→open with, at each step: exactly one blink layer, exactly two lids (no duplicate eyes), correct scaleY(0)/scaleY(1), R2 fill + Option-A geometry on close, no C2/R2 mixing, no broken images, and unchanged container/base/hair/face bounding boxes (no position/scale shift, no seam). open-before and open-after both match the SAME open baseline within tolerance (no residue). `destroy()` removes the lid layer; a C2 fallback (dark skin under opt-in) uses C2 lids (`cx 68/92`), never the R2 geometry. **(5) TESTS.** Golden captures chromium-only; DOM/functional + destroy + C2-fallback-lids + the test-handle gate check run on Chromium + Firefox + WebKit (12/12 incl. the gate test). `npm run test:unit` 69/69; existing R2 goldens (D-062/F4) + avatar-living + hub-avatar-living unchanged; the 8 blink baselines are byte-identical before/after the gate fix. Added to the manual Linux golden-regen workflow. **(6) BOUNDARIES.** **`AVATAR_R2` stays `false`** — visual test coverage + a blink-init fix, not activation. No asset, `R2_MANIFEST`, database, migration, RLS, Edge Function or pilot change. Only `js/avatar-blink-engine.js` (seam), `avatar.html` (engine-init timing fix), the new spec + baselines, the regen workflow, and this doc. **Closes F5; F2 (reproducer idempotency) and F3 (Phase-2 doc refresh) remain open.** Preserves D-032/D-033/D-042…D-062. (branch `test/avatar-r2-blink-frame-goldens`) (2026-07-22) |

| **D-064** | **Phase-2 pilot/runtime docs refreshed to the current decomposed-stack reality (closes activation-audit F3). Docs-only (2026-07-23).** F3 flagged that the pilot-onboarding and flag docs still described **Phase-1** (a single baked **PNG** base with a **static** face), which would mislead a pilot operator today — the runtime is now the **Phase-2 decomposed WebP stack** (base `body-neutral-medium-v2.webp` + face + eyes + iris + blush + hair) with **live blink** (Option-A lids, D-061/D-063) and breathing; only dynamic expressions remain off (fixed neutral raster face). **(1) REFRESHED (docs only):** `docs/167a-phase1-pilot-rollout.md` — retitled to "Avatar R2 Pilot Rollout", §1/§3/§4/§6/§7 updated for the WebP decomposed stack + live blink + D-062 atomic fallback + D-061 accepted arm-fringe debt; the 2026-07-01 Phase-1 verification is kept as **historical** and a 2026-07-22 audit re-verification (Phase-2, fixture-intercepted) added. `docs/157r-feature-flags.md` — the `AVATAR_R2` row updated (WebP stack + live blink + atomic fallback, no longer "temporary PNG preview"). `docs/AVATAR_SYSTEM.md` §9 — an additive current-status note ("Phase-2 SHIPPED under opt-in") placed above the retained Gate-progression history. **(2) ELIGIBILITY UNCHANGED:** the pilot criteria (neutral-medium only; aura/back cosmetics only) remain correct and are preserved. **(3) NOT TOUCHED:** the historical/decision docs (D-register, 167a decomposition/step-3/master-asset plans, phase-2 asset brief, ROADMAP, the `assets/avatar-r2/README.md`) correctly describe the Phase-1→Phase-2 journey **as history** and were left as-is; no historical decision rewritten. **(4) BOUNDARIES:** **docs-only** — no code, asset, `R2_MANIFEST`, database, golden, test or `AVATAR_R2` change; **`AVATAR_R2` stays `false`**. **Closes F3. F2 (arm-fringe reproducer idempotency) is now the SOLE remaining open activation-audit finding; F1/F4/F5 are closed (D-062/D-063 + R2 goldens).** Preserves D-032/D-033/D-042…D-063. | **D-065** | **Arm-fringe reproducer made idempotent/verifiable post-promotion (closes activation-audit F2 — the LAST open finding). Tool-only, no asset change (2026-07-23).** F2 flagged that `tools/avatar/build-r2-arm-fringe-fix.mjs` aborted on `main` (`exit 4`, "input hash mismatch: D-059 runtime `28765eea…`"): its runtime input-hash gate was locked to the **D-059 pre-fix** blob (`3a30d8c7…`), but `main` already carries the **D-061 promoted output** (`28765eea…`, 67,174 B). So the promoted runtime could no longer be re-derived or verified from a fresh clone without supplying the pre-fix blob from history. **(1) FIX (tool-only):** the runtime gate now accepts **either** the D-059 input **or** the D-061 output — the runtime is legitimately both the tool's input and its output. From the D-059 input it re-derives the Candidate-B fix as before; from the already-promoted D-061 output the **runtime-side selection mask is empty and the runtime candidate touches 0 px** (the arm matte is already recoloured to skin, luma < 215, so nothing re-selects), and the lossless re-encode reproduces the on-disk file **byte-for-byte**. The owner-approved **source-side exception mask is unchanged (still 2353 px, SHA `47dbec44…`)** and is reproduced byte-for-byte — it is derived from the D-057 source, not from the runtime, so the tool still legitimately prints `exception mask: 2353 px`. **(2) STRONGER SELF-VERIFICATION:** added hard-fail invariants asserting the three derived artefacts equal the tracked D-061 outputs byte-for-byte (`arm-fringe-write-exception-v1.png` `47dbec44…`, `neutral-base-v2-armfringe.png` `347a258f…`, runtime `28765eea…`) plus an explicit idempotency check (re-run over the promoted runtime must touch 0 px). Any blob that is neither the D-059 input nor the D-061 output is still rejected. **(3) VERIFIED:** on `main` the tool now runs green (`exit 0`, "idempotent verify mode"); `--promote` re-writes all three artefacts **byte-identically** → `git status assets/` stays **clean** (proven: SHAs unchanged before/after). The D-058 protect PNG and D-057 source PNG remain untouched. Provenance intact (D-061 recorded the byte-identical reproduction at promotion time; the tool can now reconfirm it from `main`). **(4) BOUNDARIES:** only `tools/avatar/build-r2-arm-fringe-fix.mjs` changed — **no asset, `R2_MANIFEST`, database, migration, RLS, Edge Function, golden, runtime-JS or pilot change; `AVATAR_R2` stays `false`.** The gitignored audit scratch (`tools/avatar/build/…`) and the vendored encoders were not committed. **Closes F2. ALL activation-audit findings F1–F5 are now closed (D-062/D-063/D-064/D-065 + R2 goldens); F6 is accepted debt, F7/F8 are LOW.** Preserves D-032/D-033/D-042…D-064. | **D-066** | **Path-aware Playwright CI — skip the full browser suite on docs-only and standalone avatar-tool-only PRs, fail-closed to full. CI-config only (2026-07-23).** After the R2 audit closed, the last several PRs were docs-only or build-tool-only yet each paid the full ~20-min (sometimes 60-min-timeout) live-backend Playwright suite for zero runtime risk. **(1) THREE MODES (pull_request only):** a tracked pure classifier `.github/ci/classify-changes.mjs` maps the PR's changed files to exactly one mode — **docs** (every file under `docs/**`), **avatar-tool** (≥1 file under `tools/avatar/**` AND every file under `tools/avatar/**` or `docs/**`), or **full** (everything else). docs → `git diff --check` + mojibake scan; avatar-tool → those + `node --check` on changed `tools/avatar/*.mjs|*.js`; full → the existing `npm ci` → browser install → global setup → whole Playwright suite, **unchanged**. **(2) SAFETY NET:** any `push` to main is **forced `full`** (classification never skips main-CI); changes to `.github/workflows/**`, `package*.json`, `playwright.config.*`, `tests/**`, `js/**`, `assets/**`, `supabase/**`, `*.html`, `*.css` — anything outside the two safe prefixes — are `full`. **(3) FAIL-CLOSED:** non-array/empty/malformed file list, an unknown path, or any classifier/import error resolves to **`full`** (over-classifying is always safe; under-classifying never is). **(4) REQUIRED CHECK PRESERVED:** the single job is still named **`test`**, still always runs and always reports in every mode (conditional *steps*, never a skipped job), so a branch-protection `test` requirement needs **no change** and a docs PR cannot be blocked by a missing/`skipped` check. **(5) IMPLEMENTATION:** official `actions/github-script@v7` paginates `pulls.listFiles` (handles >100-file PRs), imports the classifier, sets `steps.classify.outputs.mode`, logs the changed files, and writes a job summary (event/commit/files/mode/checks/skipped/reason) — **no new third-party changed-files action**. The classifier unit test (`tests/unit/ci-classify-changes.test.mjs`, 10 spec cases + fail-closed edges) runs in **every** mode, proving the fast-mode logic even on this full-mode PR. **(6) NOT CHANGED:** no app runtime, HTML/CSS, assets, goldens, database, RLS, Edge Functions or existing browser tests; timeout/retries/concurrency/secrets unchanged; **`AVATAR_R2` stays `false`.** The live-backend/concurrency split (fast modes still queue on `e2e-shared-supabase`) is intentionally **deferred to a later PR**. This PR itself edits `.github/workflows/**` → classifies as **full** and runs the whole suite one last time. Preserves D-032/D-033/D-042…D-065. (branch `ci/path-aware-playwright`) (2026-07-23) **· Follow-up fix (2026-07-24, branch `fix/ci-avatar-tool-eof`):** the avatar-tool `node --check` step used a bash `while read` loop that silently dropped the **last** file when the changed-files list had no trailing newline (`files.join('\n')` for a 1-file PR) — so a tool-only PR classified `avatar-tool` correctly and skipped Playwright, but syntax-checked **0** scripts (observed "passed on 0 changed tools/avatar script(s)" on PR #108). Fixed by (a) newline-terminating the list in the github-script writer and (b) rewriting the check in Node (`split('\n')` + `execFileSync('--check')`, like the mojibake scan), immune to the missing-newline case. Verified locally: 1 script checked both with and without a trailing newline. Workflow-only; classification rules, modes, fail-closed behaviour and the `test` required check are unchanged; `AVATAR_R2` stays `false`. |
| **D-067** | **Fast CI modes run OUTSIDE the shared Supabase concurrency lock (extends D-066). CI-config only (2026-07-24).** Problem: `playwright.yml` had **workflow-level** `concurrency: e2e-shared-supabase`, evaluated **before** file classification — so docs/avatar-tool PRs (which never start Playwright and never touch Supabase) still **queued behind full main/PR runs**, wasting minutes for zero benefit (observed repeatedly: #108/#110 fast runs sat `pending` behind the forced-full main run from the previous merge). **(1) TWO JOBS:** workflow-level concurrency removed. A new **`classify`** job (no shared lock) checks out, paginates `pulls.listFiles` via `actions/github-script@v7`, runs the tracked classifier, and outputs `mode`, `concurrency_group`, base64-encoded `changed_files`, and `reason`. The required **`test`** job now `needs: classify`, `if: always()` (so the required check still reports even if classify fails), and takes **job-level** `concurrency: ${{ needs.classify.outputs.concurrency_group || 'e2e-shared-supabase' }}` with `cancel-in-progress: false`. **(2) LOCK ROUTING** (`concurrencyGroup(mode,runId)` in `.github/ci/classify-changes.mjs`, unit-tested): **full →** `e2e-shared-supabase`; **docs/avatar-tool →** isolated `ci-fast-<run_id>` (unique per run, never blocks on the shared lock). **(3) FAIL-CLOSED to full + shared:** non-PR/push-to-main, classifier/import/API error, oversized (>3000) file list, unknown/empty mode, missing/invalid run id, or missing job output all resolve to `mode=full` + `group=e2e-shared-supabase`. A belt-and-suspenders guard forces the shared group whenever `mode==full`, and the `test` job **re-normalises** mode (fail-closed to full) before any step condition — so a full suite can **never** run under a fast group. If a fast mode's changed-files list fails to decode, the check **fails clearly** rather than escalating to a lockless full run. **(4) full stays serialized** with `update-avatar-goldens.yml` through `e2e-shared-supabase` — that workflow is **unchanged** (its shared-group contract preserved). **(5) TEST COVERAGE UNCHANGED:** docs → classifier+concurrency unit tests + `git diff --check` + mojibake; avatar-tool → + `node --check`; full → the whole existing suite (npm ci, browsers, global setup, retries, timeout, secrets) — all identical. **(6) BOUNDARIES:** only `.github/workflows/playwright.yml`, `.github/ci/classify-changes.mjs`, `tests/unit/ci-classify-changes.test.mjs`, this doc (+ ROADMAP). No app runtime, HTML/CSS, asset, golden, database, RLS, Edge Function or existing-browser-test change; **`AVATAR_R2` stays `false`.** Unit: 37/37 (22 original classifier + 15 new concurrency). This PR edits `.github/workflows/**` → classifies **full** → runs the whole suite under the shared lock (proving workflow changes stay protected). The docs-only proof (fast run starts without waiting on the main lock) is a **separate follow-up PR**. Preserves D-032/D-033/D-042…D-066. (branch `ci/fast-modes-outside-supabase-lock`) (2026-07-24) **· Hardening (pre-merge review, 2026-07-24):** four fail-closed holes closed. **(a) Partial-output safety:** the `test` job lock no longer trusts the classify `concurrency_group` output alone — the job-level `concurrency` expression derives the group from **`needs.classify.result == 'success'` AND a validated `mode` of docs/avatar-tool**, computing `ci-fast-${{ github.run_id }}` directly from the trusted run id; classify failure/cancelled/skipped, missing/invalid/full mode or partial outputs all → `e2e-shared-supabase` (mirrored by the unit-tested pure `jobLockGroup(result,mode,runId)`; the output is kept for logs only). **(b) Fast-mode payload is mandatory:** the mode-normalise step now **fails clearly** (`core.setFailed`, no fast checks, no escalation to full while on the fast lock) when `changed_files_b64` is empty, undecodable, not an array, empty, contains a non-string/empty entry, or a path escapes the mode's safe set — via the pure `validateFastPayload(mode,files)`. **(c) `>= 3000` cap:** `pulls.listFiles` returns at most 3000, so a list AT the cap may be truncated — `fileCountForcesFull(n)` now trips at `>= MAX_CHANGED_FILES (3000)` → full/shared. **(d) Output-size guard:** `outputTooLarge(len)` forces full/shared and drops the payload when the base64 exceeds `MAX_OUTPUT_B64_LEN (512 KB)` (full does not need the list). Unit: **67/67** (37 prior + 30 new hardening). Workflow-only + tests + this doc; `AVATAR_R2` stays `false`. |
| **D-068** | **D-042 expression production complete → four owner-approved expression face layers PROMOTED to the tracked Avatar R2 asset structure (dormant, no runtime change). 2026-07-24.** After the D-042 deterministic Master-composite pipeline (donor = brows/nose/mouth only; frozen Master D-032 authoritative outside the write mask; Gate A/B/D + owner visual sign-off) produced the positive expression set, the owner accepted **proud v1, curious v1, focused v2, determined v2** and rejected **focused v1** (too stern/sour) and **determined v1** (too sly/aggressive, crooked smirk). **(1) DETERMINISTIC ALLOWLIST PROMOTION:** new tool `tools/avatar/promote-expression-layers.mjs` SHA-pins the exact four approved sources and hard-fails on unknown expression / wrong version / wrong source-SHA / wrong dims / missing alpha / wrong destination / the two rejected v1 SHAs / a changed Master / a changed `AVATAR_R2` — so no rejected or wrong candidate can be selected by directory order or timestamp. It encodes via the existing `encode-webp.mjs --half` (identical `-q 90 -alpha_q 100 -m 6 -metadata none -resize 512 0` contract as the six neutral layers, D-018), double-encodes for determinism, and verifies each output is 512×768 with alpha. **(2) FOUR TRACKED WEBP ASSETS** under `assets/avatar-r2/face/` (source→output SHA): `face-proud-v1.webp` (src `2B8D1453…` → `8BDED8BE…`, 3,336 B), `face-curious-v1.webp` (`C76F57DF…` → `CC1CA222…`, 3,848 B), `face-focused-v2.webp` (`C19BFF26…` → `D71F3FEA…`, 2,736 B), `face-determined-v2.webp` (`1F7569AC…` → `21F0665F…`, 3,150 B); total ≈12.8 KB (≪ ADR-163D budget). **privacy boundary** forbidding all PII (name/email/UID/session/token/quiz/progression/cosmetics/localStorage/raw errors/stack traces). **(6) TEST STRATEGY (later PR):** unit (one event per outcome, none without opt-in, throwing helper doesn't affect render, no PII, dedup, blink/breathing/expr emit nothing, C2 default unchanged) + one self-served, fixture-intercepted Playwright spec via `page.on("console")` (no backend, no shared-student mutation). **(7) NON-GOALS:** no dashboard/analytics/Supabase table/Edge Function/beacon/retention/tracking/fingerprint/flag-service/allowlist/UI-toggle/auto-classification. **(8) BOUNDARIES:** docs-only (`167a-r2-pilot-observability-design.md` new, `167a-phase1-pilot-rollout.md` additive ref, this register, `ROADMAP.md`); | **D-082** | **Remaining-slot completion audit (neck/torso/body) — the recorded `neck → torso → body` plan is NOT executable: neck and body have NO catalog items, and the one torso item CANNOT be re-seated onto the R2 figure. Read-only audit + docs only; owner decision required (2026-07-30).** **(1) LIVE-CATALOG PREFLIGHT (read-only `select`, no write/migration/schema change):** per slot_type — aura 4, back 3, headwear 5, face 3, eyes 1, **`neck` 0**, **`torso` 1**, **`body` 0** (no active/enabled/status column, so no active-status is claimed). **`neck` and `body` have no items at all** → nothing to wire, re-seat or golden; a slice for either would add gate config + tests for zero user-visible content. They are closed as **`NO_CATALOG_ITEMS`** (body's prior `BLOCKED_BY_RUNTIME_CHANGE` classification stands and is now additionally moot). The single torso item is **`armor-knight`** ("Ridderdragt", `/assets/avatar/shirt/armor-knight.svg`, layer_order 2, 300 coins). **(2) MEASUREMENT METHOD:** both figures measured in the SAME space — the C2 authoring canvas `viewBox 0 0 160 240`. R2: the tracked base raster `body-neutral-medium-v1.png` (512×768) alpha-decoded and normalized (scale 0.3125), solid silhouette `alpha >= 128` (AA ramp excluded, per D-071); C2 + item geometry read directly from `body-neutral-medium-c2.svg` and `armor-knight.svg`. Scripts were **scratchpad-only — no `tools/avatar/` addition, nothing written into the repo.** **(3) R2 SILHOUETTE:** bbox x 40.3–118.4, y 5.6–235.9; neck narrowest y≈83 (width 21.9); shoulders begin y≈87; **torso band y 92–160 = x 48.8–110.6 as ONE connected run — the R2 arms lie AGAINST the body**; leg split (crotch) y≈160; legs at y 178 = x 57.5–101.3 (width 43.8). Edge widths: y92 48.8 · y106 55.6 · y119 60.6 · y134 60.9 · y150 60.9 · y160 61.9. **(4) THE ITEM TRACKS THE C2 ARM POSE PRECISELY** (armour arm plates x 12–34/126–148 vs C2 arms x 14–40/120–146; C2 hands cx 27/133): the C2 figure holds its arms OUT clear of the torso, the R2 Master figure holds them DOWN against the body — **a pose difference, not an offset.** **(5) ALPHA PROBE (decisive):** R2 solid coverage inside the item's own rectangles — left arm plate **0 px**, right arm plate **0 px**, left pauldron **0 px**, right pauldron 3 px (grazing edge), left elbow guard **0 px**, right elbow guard **0 px**, both C2 hand discs 0 px; chest plate 70.1 % (50,633 px), belt 46.5 % (2,977 px), reference R2 torso band x48–111/y92–160 94.7 %. **Every arm-side element of the armour lands on EMPTY canvas** → rendered as-is, two detached slabs of metal would float beside the figure. **(6) CLASSIFICATION `NEEDS_R2_SPECIFIC_ASSET` — CONFIRMED AS MEASURED FACT (was an estimate in D-079):** the item needs **two mutually exclusive** uniform horizontal scales about x=80 (measured at y=134) — **0.46** to seat the arm plates on the R2 arms (x 12 → R2 edge 49.1) vs **0.76** to fit the chest plate to the R2 torso (half-width 40 → 30.5). At 0.46 the chest plate shrinks to width 36.8 on a 60.9-wide torso (**≈12 units of bare skin per side**); at 0.76 the pauldrons/arm plates sit **up to ≈21 units OUTSIDE the silhouette** (still floating). Vertically independent and equally structural: the belt (y 174–182) sits **below the R2 crotch (y≈160)** and overhangs the R2 legs by **≈18 units per side** at y 178. **So the wrapper-transform mechanism that carried slices 1–3 (D-079/D-080/D-081) does NOT extend to this slot** — a torso cosmetic on R2 requires art drawn for the R2 silhouette + arm pose (AI is permitted here per D-034: shop/cosmetic overlays; D-033 rejects AI only for base/rig). **(7) LIVE PILOT DEFECT FOUND WHILE AUDITING — severity MAJOR (D-072 vocabulary):** `composeR2Layers` gates cosmetics via `.filter(... isR2SupportedCosmeticSlot(c.slot))`, and `torso` is absent from `R2_SUPPORTED_COSMETIC_SLOTS`, so **an equipped Ridderdragt is dropped SILENTLY** — no layer, no warning, no fallback. In the pilot that is CURRENTLY RUNNING (D-078, Wave 1), a student who paid 300 coins sees the armour on C2 and **nothing at all on R2**. No data loss (`equipped_slots` untouched; opt-out restores it), but the avatar silently contradicts the shop and the student's inventory. This is NOT raster fidelity (D-071) and NOT covered by D-077 (shop grid); it is the first case where the R2 slot-gate is user-visible as **missing content**, and it will recur for every future torso item. **(8) OPTIONS + RECOMMENDATION (owner decision required):** **A** produce an R2-specific torso asset (art first, then a wiring PR — the only option that puts armour on R2; must read correctly at D-071 render sizes 180×270 down to 52×78, painting over the arms baked into the base raster); **B** force whole-avatar C2 while a torso item is equipped (small runtime PR over the existing tested `forceC2` path — closes the defect with no new art; those students see no R2 while equipped; consistent with D-077's reasoning); **C** defer + document (cheapest, knowingly leaves a MAJOR defect in a running pilot); **D** retire/re-slot the item (**not recommended** — an owned paid item; product regression + touches purchase history). **RECOMMENDED: B now, A later if the owner wants armour on R2** — and close neck/body as `NO_CATALOG_ITEMS` rather than carrying them as pending roadmap work. Track would then close as: aura/back/headwear/eyes/face **wired** · torso **handled by B, upgradeable by A** · neck/body **closed, no content**. **(9) BOUNDARIES:** read-only audit; **nothing executed, changed or produced** — no runtime/asset/`R2_MANIFEST`/golden/test/migration/RLS/Edge-Function/workflow change, no image generation, no catalog change; Master untouched; **`AVATAR_R2` stays `false`**; **pilot status stays `PILOT_WAVE_1_IN_PROGRESS`**; participant log unchanged; no user onboarded; no student identifier recorded. Doc: `docs/167a-r2-cosmetic-slot-completion-audit.md` (`OWNER_DECIDED_B`). **(10) OWNER DECISION (2026-07-31): option B** — force whole-avatar C2 while a torso item is equipped, via the existing tested `forceC2` path. Option A (R2-specific torso asset) is deferred as separate art work, not rejected; `neck`/`body` are closed as `NO_CATALOG_ITEMS`. **B is implemented in a SEPARATE runtime PR — this entry and its PR remain docs-only** (no runtime/asset/test/golden/catalog change; `AVATAR_R2` stays `false`; pilot status stays `PILOT_WAVE_1_IN_PROGRESS`). D-071…D-081 not rewritten. Preserves D-032/D-033/D-042…D-081. (branch `docs/avatar-r2-slot-completion-audit-d082`) (2026-07-30; decision recorded 2026-07-31) |
| **D-083** | **D-082 option B IMPLEMENTED — an equipped cosmetic the R2 stack cannot render now drops the WHOLE avatar to C2 instead of vanishing. Closes the MAJOR live pilot defect (2026-07-31).** **(1) DEFECT (D-082 §6):** `composeR2Layers` filtered unsupported slots (neck/torso/body) out of the R2 stack **silently** — no layer, no warning, no fallback — so an opted-in pilot student who paid 300 coins for the Ridderdragt (`armor-knight`, the only live `torso` item) saw the armour on C2 and **nothing at all on R2**. **(2) MECHANISM (additive, GENERIC BY SLOT — not an armor-knight special case):** new pure helpers in `js/avatar-layers.js` — `r2UnrenderableCosmeticSlots(cosmetics)` (distinct unsupported slots present, encounter order) + `r2RequiresC2Fallback(cosmetics)`; `composeR2Layers` returns **null** when any equipped cosmetic is unrenderable on R2 (checked FIRST, before the stack resolve — the decision depends only on what is equipped), so the existing whole-stack-or-C2 fallback renders the COMPLETE C2 path with the item visible on its proven C2 anchors. Renderability uses the SAME criteria the composer already applied (`src` present + numeric `z`), so a malformed entry — which would not render on C2 either — never forces a healthy avatar off R2.
